## Red team: Roadmap and Time lenses, checkpoint 3

Lenses run: Roadmap and Time, the two the brief names for a roadmap. Skipped: the other five, because a roadmap's claims are order, placement and dates. Mode: reviewer. Two Sonnet 5.5 readers, one per lens, each a separate session started from the command line with read-only tools and the scheduler, reading this file, the line, the area files and the scheduler fresh. The author of the subject is the roadmap session, a Sonnet 5.5 session too, so the readers share the author's model and blind spots; the checkpoint 3 reviewers on a different model remain the independent attack. Eighteen findings, sixteen serious and two minor in the readers' own ranking (thirteen on the Roadmap lens, five on Time). I fixed what could be fixed in the plan and say below what I could not. The strongest claim written before each pass: Roadmap, "no epic starts before what it needs, and each milestone means what it says"; Time, "the dates survive a person away, the freeze and the calendar's real waits". The check I ran myself (the scheduler's check of every start against its dependencies) held, and the Roadmap reader confirmed it; their findings are all about what that check does not hold.

### Fixed

| Reader | Finding | What changed |
|---|---|---|
| Roadmap | The "same or better" soak ran before six Saboteer-class epics finished, and Corsairs had none | R7b is split by game: Saboteer's soak (3 gates) waits for every Saboteer-tagged part and Corsairs' (R7c, 2 gates) for its last; each milestone date is the day its own soak ends |
| Roadmap | The one-editor guarantee had holes: the festival gates and the soak carried no tag | The festival gates, R7b, R7c, UI3, NW4a and WF5a carry tags; MV now starts the day after the merge gate. CL2, W2a, W4c and LV3a are seeds without a tag by design (package work) and the file says so |
| Roadmap | The floor compile and the 6.3 row were said to start at the 6.7 move but were dated 2028 | LV3a and LV7a are Saboteer's class and LV7a waits on MV (it now lands 2027-05-24); until then the row is run by hand (assumption 10) |
| Roadmap | The chart fragment was the 2b plan and the decisions were pending | Regenerated from the scheduler; four decisions written |
| Roadmap | Core's section and ground vocabulary were built after R0 and W1a, which use them | R0 and W1a wait on L2c |
| Roadmap | The demo was reported done on 2026-11-18 though the Steam deploy is a human step | The milestone row says the files and the tag script are ready on 2026-11-18 and the deploy is the developer's on 2027-02-03 |
| Roadmap | LV7 carried the undated gates' tokens twice | An epic with undated parts is priced on its dated parts |
| Roadmap | UI1 and UI4a land content in Studio packages that L11c and L11b create later | UI1 and UI4a create the Studio.Presentation and Studio.Online package folders; L11c and L11b are what remains (reconciliation 24) |
| Roadmap | GeoForge.Networking is claimed by NW2 and W2; R2's held pieces have no story after NW7 | Listed as claims (reconciliation 25) and in the backlog table |
| Roadmap | "No Entities in the game" is claimed before Core stops requiring Entities | The milestone says Saboteer's own code; the package leaves at UN4b in the Corsairs milestone (reconciliation 27, a claim to Unity) |
| Time | The Next Fest "yes" has no Steam waits, no cover for a person away and no defect gate before the press preview | A fourth festival gate on 2027-02-09, the sprint's reserve day behind the first gate, Steam's own steps under calendar items and in decision 3 |
| Time | A late Unity 6.7 was never run | A sensitivity row (days) and decision 4 |
| Time | The steady-state budget was a copy of MV and left out update releases and upstream drift | Two lines added (22 gates, 4.2 million tokens), the runtime-change hedge named, FL (2 gates, 2027-12-13) put in the plan, rule 17's timing stated |
| Time | Leave and holidays were absent from the headline and the "person away" rows could not show an absence | The 0.93 dates sit beside the headline and rows cover the festival run-up, sprint 10 and Christmas 2027, each taking the worse stream |

### Could not fix

- **The MV order is a priority and not a dependency.** If 6.7 is late, L3 and L4 rewrite on 6.4 and MV moves them again; that second pass is unpriced. Making L3 wait for 6.7 would tie Saboteer's path to Unity's release date. Decision 4 asks.
- **Saboteer's compile gain waits for the Corsairs milestone** because Core carries the Entities package until UN4b, which waits behind the world chain (UN3, UN4a, UN5). I did not move UN4b earlier; the claim is with Unity.
- **Steam's own rules** are in no file I read, so the build date of 2027-02-04 and the freeze are assumptions (decision 3).
- **Dates move by days when an input changes**, because a started item is not preempted; the sensitivity table is a set of runs, not a prediction of the next one.
- **Overlaps counted as filed.** The Entities removal in W1, W2 and UN6, GeoForge.Networking in NW2 and W2, and the floor compile in WF3 and LV7 may be counted twice, up to about 10 gates.
- **My splits and sizings** (R1b, R2, R3, R6, R7b, R8, the parts of W1, W2, W6, W7, W9, UN1, UN4, LV1, LV3, LV5, WF2 to WF5 and CL6, L6, L3's collision story, L11's house set) are mine; the owners receive each as a claim.
- **The gate rate and the token rates** are the decided and the 2b ones, unmeasured until sprint 1.
- **The readers shared the author's model.**

Could not check: whether each area's story counts are right (I priced the files as filed), whether Unity 6.7 LTS ships on the decided date, Steam's festival steps, and PurrNet or Unity behaviour (nothing here was run).

### Independent readers, checkpoint 3

Eleven findings from Opus 5.5 readers, one lens each (Stranger, Designer and artist, Performance, Roadmap), against the plan this file held before this pass. I checked each claim against the file, the scheduler and the code or manifest it names. Eight are fixed, one is fixed in part, and two are fixed with one part rejected. None changes the amended line. I re-ran the scheduler once with all the changes together, so every date in this file comes from that one run. The reconciliations 28 to 36 hold the changes; the new epics are SFp and LM.

1. **The strangers run only after both games (serious, Stranger). Fixed.** The reader is right. Living.md says CoreOnly is L1's acceptance test and every other project turns on with the epics that deliver its packages; the plan held the whole harness behind UN4b and called it after both games, so a boundary a stranger breaks would have been found after two games depended on it. LV5 is split by package (reconciliation 34). Only the Core-only compile without Entities (LV5m) waits for UN4b, because Core keeps that package until then. The harness and the Core-only project run 2027-03-11 to 2027-03-12; the card game compiles against Core and UI on 2027-05-19 and runs its scenario on 2027-10-19; the farming sim runs 2027-10-20 to 2027-10-22 and the MMO server's first build 2027-10-25, all inside Saboteer's class and before the milestone. Inventory's raw writers close (R6b) only after the farming sim has harvested through the public surface, which is the kind of break the reader meant. What stays after both: the racing and underwater strangers (they need Corsairs' packages) and the Matrix. Two limits: I gave the card game a compile step but not the farming sim or the MMO server, whose packages land between March and October 2027 and get one step each, because a gate per package costs more than it finds; and a stranger shows what a project without Saboteer's setup shows, so what only Saboteer's setup shows is R7b's job.

2. **For five months the designer's tool and Saboteer's floors read different data (serious, Designer and artist). Fixed.** The reader says the gap comes from capacity placement and not from dependencies, and that is what the scheduler showed. W10 is now scheduled ahead of every other Saboteer-tagged part the day W4b ends: W4b ends 2027-07-22 and W10 runs 2027-07-26 to 2027-08-09. The gap between authoring a place in the new editor and seeing it in a Saboteer floor is those 18 days. Until W10 ends the floors still come from Pro's pipeline and the old captures, which W4b leaves in place and W10 retires, so nobody authors twice for more than a sprint.

3. **The gym profile's studio character is built before Character exists (serious, Designer and artist). Fixed.** The reader is right: L11d1 needed only W4b and L11t. It now waits on L4 and R5a as well (the packaged character and its hold pose), and W4c waits on L11d1, so its held-item story inherits both. The commands that grant abilities are L11d2's, which already waits on L11a1 (Studio.Gameplay). Line.md's Studio.Content row names Places, Character and Studio.Gameplay as one list; splitting it between L11d1 and L11d2 is a claim to Studio.Content's owner. Cost: W4c now ends 2027-08-18; it ended 2027-06-15 in the earlier dates, and most of the difference is the game days and W10 going first.

4. **For four months the art window passes a held prop that the games still hold wrong (serious, Designer and artist). Fixed in part.** The reader is right that a prop which passes the grip check and is sent sits at its pivot while both games align held items with HeldGrip, placed by hand on 27 prefabs. Two changes. Send (A5) now waits on A4, and the grip check runs only in a project that has ArtPipeline installed, which a game gets in A4, so no game sees the check before it reads grip nodes. And A4 moved into Saboteer's class with the two epics it waits on (A1a and A2, 21 gates with A4), and R7b waits on it, so the soak measures the rebuilt prefabs; it costs three days on Saboteer's date. A4 runs 2028-01-04 to 2028-01-10, not next to A2 (2027-07-02 to 2027-07-14), because Saboteer's editor is busy in between; what matters for the artist is that A4 comes before Send (2028-01-19 to 2028-02-01). Not fixed: A4 only starts Corsairs, so a prop sent to Corsairs still sits at its pivot until Corsairs' prefabs follow. That is listed under "Could not fix" below.

5. **Saboteer has no frame-time baseline (serious, Performance). Fixed.** The reader is right. R7a gains a story: the game's own FrameCostTrace already records frame, main-thread, render-thread and GPU time and the worst frame through Frame Timing Stats; the story fixes the Areas scenario and the reference PC, runs it as host and as client, reads memory through the same recorder path, and stores the figures beside the soak counts before Diagnostics replaces the trace. R7b reruns it on Diagnostics' costs and compares. R7a runs 2027-01-06 to 2027-01-11, before L3 starts rewriting the game (2027-03-12). One thing I have not verified: whether the trace's counters give the total memory in use; the story's first check settles it, and if they do not the story names the counter that does. R7a is now four stories and R7b four; both are my sizing, a claim to Runtime.

6. **Saboteer loses its crew log relay and the replacement waits on a spike dated last (serious, Roadmap). Fixed by keeping the relay.** The reader is right, and I checked the code: CrewLogRelay sends the log tally by ServerRpc and the log file by SyncRawFile on an error, has no build guard and sits on the player prefab, so it works in every build; Diagnostics' black box is editor and development builds only, and the shipped recorder, file and relay wait for NW7. NW4b now retires the two ChatNetwork classes and leaves the relay, and it no longer waits on R2b. The reader's other option, running NW7 right after NW2 and pricing the recorder, is decision 7; I have not priced the recorder because the spike decides its size. A claim to Networking and Runtime.

7. **Every stranger project is held until after both games (serious, Roadmap). Fixed with finding 1.** Same cause and same change.

8. **L1 and LV1b change Saboteer's packages ten weeks before the demo build, untagged (serious, Roadmap). Fixed, one part rejected.** The reader is right, and the manifest shows it: Saboteer follows Core on `#dev`, GeoForge on `#dev_1.0` and the other nine on their default branch, and no tag exists before the build. SFp (one gate, 2026-11-02) pins every line to a commit and names one demo branch per package for the fixes of SFd and SFb; L1, LV1b and WF7a wait for it. LM edits Corsairs' lines for the layout move, and MV's fifth story takes Saboteer onto the new layout after the festival merge. Rejected: tagging L1 and LV1b with the games' tags. They edit package repos and never open a game, so a tag would hold Saboteer's editor for eleven gates for nothing; the risk was the manifest, and the pin removes it. Decision 5 asks the developer.

9. **The one-editor rule fills both games with line work for a year (serious, Roadmap). Fixed in the plan; the answer is the developer's.** The reader is right that the games' own work was in no stream. I added game days: each game keeps a Wednesday every second week with no line part holding its tag (46 days), and re-ran the dates. It costs five weeks on Saboteer (2028-02-24 against 2028-01-19 with none) and the model idles the stream that day, so it is an upper bound. The sensitivity table shows none and one a week. Decision 6 asks whether to keep them.

10. **Test rows for 6000.3 and 6000.4 are built months after the plan drops them (minor, Roadmap). Fixed.** A6 and LV5 now name the rows line rule 5 names on the day they run: the floor 6000.3 and the studio's editor until the move (MV), 6000.3 and 6000.7 until the floor moves (FL, 2027-12-13), and 6000.7 alone after. A6 runs 2028-08-02 to 2028-08-03, so its pair test is on 6000.7 plus the canary editor; LV5c and LV5d run after FL too. Living.md still says "on 6000.3 and 6000.4" in its Matrix row; a claim to Living.

11. **UN6 and WF7b sit in Saboteer's serial tag although its milestone gets nothing from them (minor, Roadmap). Fixed, one part rejected.** The reader is right and I checked it: Core keeps the Entities package in its manifest until UN4b, so Saboteer compiles Entities at its milestone either way, and WF7b swaps tooling. Both are Corsairs' class now, wait for R7b, and R7b no longer lists them; R7c waits for both and repeats Saboteer's soak once, because UN6 edits Saboteer's code after R7b. Saboteer's milestone no longer claims it has no Entities in its own code. Rejected: placing UN6 beside UN4b. UN6 edits Saboteer, whose editor is free after its soak, and UN4b is 2028-06-06 to 2028-06-12, so waiting for it would remove no work. A claim to Unity: its text says Saboteer sheds Entities when U6 lands.

**Could not fix, from these readers.**

- A prop sent to Corsairs sits at its pivot until Corsairs' prefabs read the contract; A4 only starts that half (finding 4).
- The game days are an assumption until decision 6 is answered, and the model prices them as an idle stream, so the five weeks are an upper bound (finding 9).
- Whether FrameCostTrace's counters give the total memory in use is unverified (finding 5).
- The size of a shipped recorder and relay is not priced; the prediction spike decides it (finding 6).
- The readers' model is the same family for all of them and I am Sonnet 5.5: a shared blind spot is possible on both sides.

### The seven decisions of checkpoint 3 and the developer's answers of 2026-10-24

| # | Question | Answer |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Stop changing Saboteer's code for the four weeks before the demo build | Yes; the freeze runs from mid January (question 13) |
| 2 | Two people in the same game project at once | Yes; the one-person rule leaves the roadmap (question 15) |
| 3 | Check Steam's partner page for the festival dates | Yes, before 14 December (question 17) |
| 4 | L3 and L4 wait for Unity 6.7 if it is late | No; start on 6.4 and move when it ships (question 18) |
| 5 | Build the demo from package versions fixed on 2 November | No; nothing is pinned (question 14) |
| 6 | A game day every second week | No game days (question 16) |
| 7 | Saboteer keeps its crew log relay until a shipped recorder exists | Yes (question 19) |

## Record: the red teams of the 2b plan

Kept as the record. Its dates, milestone names and epic ids belong to the 2b plan; the answers above replace them.

### Red team: Roadmap and Time lenses

Lenses run: Roadmap and Time, the two the brief names for a roadmap. Skipped: the other six, because a roadmap's claims are order, placement and dates. Mode: reviewer. Each reader was a separate Sonnet 5.5 session started from the command line with read-only tools, reading this file and the area files fresh; the author of the subject is the roadmap session, so the reader is a different session but not a different model. Two rounds: the first found 21 findings and the second, after the fixes, 14 more. Before the first round I had already found and fixed two of my own (the golden hashes and baseline soak placed after the first rewrite, and the CL5 and LV4 loop), and both are in the reconciliations. The strongest claim under each lens, written before the pass: Roadmap, "no epic starts before what it needs and the milestones mean what they say"; Time, "the dates survive a person away and the calendar's real waits".

#### Fixed

| Round | Severity | Finding | What changed |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 Roadmap | Serious | W4c and NV5 draw their editors from Core.Editor's controls (UI7) but were placed before it | UI7 split; UI7a (controls and report view) gates W4c, NV5 and A1b |
| 1 Roadmap | Serious | NW4 retires both ChatNetwork classes a month before Switchboard's bridge exists | NW4 split; NW4b waits for UI4b and R2b |
| 1 Roadmap and Time | Serious | M2 promised a store listing nothing could produce; the second round found the same flaw moved to M3 | Listing is an M4 event with its prerequisites named as M4 members; M2 and M3 promise only the pilot beta and the final-tag mechanism |
| 1 Roadmap | Serious | M3 said both games on Networking while Corsairs' modules were retired in M4 | NW3b is an M3 member |
| 1 Roadmap | Serious | Studio.Gameplay, the line's first probe of Inventory's and Modifiers' seams, ran after those seams shipped | L11a1 runs straight after the pitch and L6, ahead of R6 and UI5; it cannot run before L6 exists, so a gap it finds reopens L6 as one story |
| 1 Roadmap | Serious | A2's ragdoll dry run lives in R5, which is backlog | R5a (one gate) scheduled; A2 depends on it |
| 1 Roadmap | Serious | CL8's dependencies could not run its own scenario | CL8 also waits for CL6e, W4c and NV5 |
| 1 Roadmap and 2 Roadmap | Serious and minor | Dependencies the area files state were missing (LV5 strangers, W6b on W1a, NV2a on R3a, NW3b on UN4a, WF7 on A1b, W6a on WF2a, L7a, L8 and UI1 on the migration script, CL3 on CL6a) | Added; the scheduler's check now covers them |
| 1 Roadmap | Minor | MV scheduled after the rewrites and its delay misstated | MV now waits for UN8 on purpose and the actual delay is computed in reconciliation 16 |
| 1 Roadmap and 2 Roadmap | Minor | Milestone "gates so far" applied two rules; the sensitivity rows disagreed at M3; the pitch price ignored the fan-out; "next chains are shorter" was false; "each 0.25 lost costs six months" held once | One rule (every gate finished by the date); the two rows explained; pitch priced at 1.3 million; chains computed by the program and shown as ties; both ends of the rate range stated |
| 1 Time | Serious | The "one person away" row left M1 unchanged | The row takes the worse of the two streams; M1 moves by up to two weeks |
| 1 Time | Serious | The reserve count and the Christmas weeks of 2027 were stated wrongly | the reserve count is computed from the calendar instead of typed; the 2027 off days are listed |
| 1 Time | Serious | Publisher enrolment had no lead time and the layout decision's date broke the stated rule | LV1b's uploader probe starts 2026-11-16, enrolment is recommended by 2026-10-30 with 2026-11-09 as the latest; decision 1 says why the week of notice does not apply |
| 2 Roadmap | Blocker | M3's first final tag preceded the Editor gate, the strangers and the soak a final tag requires | First listing moved to M4 with the Editor gate (WF3b), the card game (LV5b1), the other strangers and matrix (LV5c, LV5d), the tag gate (LV7b) and the docs and support routine (WF6b) as members |
| 2 Roadmap | Serious | The player test for operation discovery under stripping ran a year after the old console was deleted | UN1c (one gate) runs before CL3 and CL3 depends on it |
| 2 Time | Serious | Leave and holidays were admitted at 7 percent of capacity but priced nowhere, and nothing was held for fixing what the rounds find | A rate row at 0.93; FX, 18 gates after each milestone round and the audit |
| 2 Time | Serious | End of 6.3 LTS support, the 6.7 date, Asset Store review and enrolment were not on any list | A section of calendar items outside the gate count |
| 2 Time | Serious | WF8, the Unity release review, was scheduled after the end of 6.3 support | WF8 is an M2 member |
| 2 Roadmap | Serious | CL3 waits for WorldObjects' operations, which no epic named | CL3 depends on CL6a; if that story is larger than one gate it is a claim back to the command line area |

#### Could not fix

- **Whether the first listing needs the whole of M4's preconditions.** The Workflow area ties a final tag to the strangers status and the Editor gate; I followed it. If the developer is willing to list on a lighter bar (a beta listing, or the tag without the matrix), the listing moves back to M3 and the area's rule changes. That is a decision for the Workflow area and the developer, not for the roadmap.
- **The ragdoll in the fault matrix.** R5 stays backlog because nothing on the line waits for it, so R7b cannot exercise the ragdoll driver. Either R5 comes in (seven gates) or Character's status says the ragdoll is excluded; `Runtime.md` contradicts itself here and the claim is back with its owner.
- **Asset Store review time** is not stated in any file and I did not measure it. The plan says to submit a sprint early and cannot say more.
- **Leave and illness** are a rate, not a date. The plan cannot know who is away when.
- **Story-level dependencies inside a part** (for example, which of W2a's nine stories W6b actually needs) are not modelled; a part waits for its dependencies as a whole, which is conservative and can only make a start later than needed.
- **The gate rate itself** is the decided one and unproven until sprint 1; every date after M1 scales with it.
- **The readers were the same model as the author**, and the checkpoint 3 reviewers on a different model remain the independent attack.

Could not check: whether each area's story counts are right (I priced the files as filed), whether Unity 6.7 LTS ships on the decided date, and PurrNet or Unity behaviour (nothing here was run).

### Red team: the whole-pitch round

Ten independent readers attacked the whole pitch and ten findings landed on the roadmap. Each answer is in plain words: what I changed, what I rejected, what I could not fix.

**1. Persistence has no design for the MMO save and crash-atomic trades (serious). Fixed in the plan, not designed.** The reader is right: L8 was priced from revision 4's epic and no area designed the shard-facing half. I added PP, a pitch that answers the transactional unit across two containers' sections, per-entity records on login, partial region loads and a database implementation of the storage seam that never blocks a tick, and L8 now waits for it. L8b (6 gates, a placeholder) builds the durable trade after R6a. The first sale does not wait for L8b, because PP runs before R6a and whatever Inventory must expose for a durable trade goes into R6a's receipts before listing, not after. Not fixed: the design itself. PP produces it, and L8's own sentence stays unchanged until PP returns.

**2. The MMO's boundary decisions wait for a pitch never scheduled while ids are persisted from M3 (serious). Fixed in part.** I took both suggestions. PM, a three-gate pitch on ids, handoff, account identity and persistence keys, runs before L3 freezes the id shape. Decision 8 binds meanwhile: a persisted or replicated id carries no participant slot and no authority-local part (assumed yes, needed by 2026-11-30). Not fixed: the MMO's own shard pitch (handoff mechanics, server-side movement authority, plan by region) stays unscheduled, because I cannot price a shard design before PM says what it must not undo. It is on the "not scheduled" list by name now. The id form itself is World's and Runtime's to hold: that goes to them as a finding.

**3. Agent context operations are built twice (minor). Fixed.** CL5 owns `core context`, `core context-install` and the notes install. LV4 keeps the file contract, the names-resolve and hash checks (Living's A3 and A4), the snippet runner, sample import and sync, the smoke run and the docs convention. Living spells the operations differently (`core_context`, `core_agent_notes`); that goes back to Living to use CommandLine's names.

**4. Inventory's first listing waits on packages it never installs, and its stranger runs after the listing (serious). Fixed.** The tag gate is now per package (LV7b for Inventory's list, LV7c for the rest). Inventory's list is the card game, the farming sim without Places (split from its village step), a first build of the MMO server (new, 1 gate) and the Inventory slice of the soak. Navigation, Horizon and the Studio parts are out of the chain. All of it runs before the listing, which moves from M4 to about 2027-10-25. The card game installs no Inventory by `Line.md`'s table, so it is not in Inventory's list, although milestone 9 of the reconciliations cited it; LV5b1 still runs in the same week because the release train (WF5) waits on it.

**5. UN4b is twelve weeks after UN5 and Corsairs is rewired in the gap (serious). Fixed.** UN4b runs straight after UN5 as one unit, and the manifest story moved to UN4b's last story, so UN5's outcome is reached when UN4b ends. Every Corsairs-tagged epic (R3b, R4, W2b) starts after it.

**6. Three dates are claimed for Core without Entities (serious). Fixed, by choosing the late date.** One date: the end of UN4b (2027-06-28). L1's and L3's value lines say so, LV5a waits for UN4b and re-measures, and WF4a's pilot install time is labelled "with Entities". The other suggestion, moving ECS out of Core in L3 (Unity direction A as a stopgap), I rejected: it writes a home for the same code in WorldObjects.Entities and deletes it again in UN4 and UN5, so the early date costs stories nothing prices, and it buys nothing a customer sees, because the first buyer exists from 2027-10-25. Not fixed: Living.md line 249 repeats the L1 claim; that goes to Living's owner.

**7. Studio clusters arrive 9 to 14 months after the epics that evict their contents (serious). Fixed, with one change to the line.** L11t is before UI1. UI1 gains one story that lands the chrome and the theme in Studio.Presentation, and UI4a one that lands the profile in Studio.Online. The catch: Studio.Online's line row hard-depends on Networking, which does not exist in March 2027. I set its first version to Switchboard only, with Networking and Diagnostics as bridges when L11b lands. That changes a row of `Line.md`, so it is decision 11 and the developer's.

**8. Equipment is built on raw writers one month before R6 removes them (serious). Fixed.** R6 splits. R6a is the public transaction surface, and L11a1 builds equipment on it. R6b makes the writers internal afterwards. The first probe now tests the surface that ships. If the probe finds that receipts lack something equipment needs, it reopens R6a, not R6b.

**9. Saboteer's Next Fest has no date, freeze or branch (serious). Fixed in part.** SF holds a pinned-tag branch for the festival build, a four-week hold on Saboteer-tagged rewires and three gates per festival, and decision 9 asks for the dates. Not fixed: no file I read has the dates, so no freeze window is on the calendar, and the three gates are my assumption, not a measurement. The saved-layout reset at UI6 and UI4a is named in SF so it is a decision to ship.

**10. Two late answers force renames and rework (minor). Fixed.** Decision 7 is needed by 2027-02-18, before UI4a creates the repo. Core 1.0 is a scheduled event (CT) before the first listing, with Core's audit (AUc) before it. Not fixed: nothing stops Core changing after 1.0; the rule is that it changes additively until a line release, and CL7's tabs and later fixes are minors.

#### Could not fix, whole-pitch round

- **The scheduler was not re-run.** The program is not in the Workflow folder. The re-placed items are hand-placed and every later date is an estimate; the sprint table and chart show the earlier run.
- **Persistence and the shard are not designed**, only scheduled. PP exists to do that.
- **Saboteer's festival dates** are unknown to me.
- **The size of L8b and SF** are placeholders until PP returns and sprint 1 measures the rate.
- **Whether the first listing needs the MMO server's first build** is my reading of "the strangers that install Inventory"; the Workflow area owns the rule.
